Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any algebras module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 322. The projective resolution has length at most 206 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by algebras generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 322, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 206. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a algebras algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 507. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 247/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 507 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the algebras structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 247. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
